HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_175022a6ed954cabee38","text":"96:11–14 confirmed that the servant’s prayer belongs to the guidance-and-seeing relation rather than to the restrainer’s jurisdiction. The surprise was 96:15: coercive forelock seizure reversed the attempted subduing and activated both `ع ب د:B005` and, more tentatively, the snare branch `ص ل و:B004`. That later capture made the distinction between chosen service and imposed humiliation structurally visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_51cba3f211d1416c399d","text":"2. **Prayer as both prescribed act and communicative appeal.** `ص ل و:B003` supplies prescribed worship with bodily limits, while `ص ل و:B002` supplies supplication, praise, blessing, and mercy. Guidance and protective caution follow in 96:11–12, and divine seeing appears in 96:14. The prescribed branch gives the act its embodied discipline; the supplication branch makes it a directed communication under pressure. I infer that restraining the act attempts to interrupt both bodily worship and appeal. `صلى` thus reads as a complete relation, not a gesture isolated from speech and mercy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B002","root_000879/B004","root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6ef255d2b86444879bdf","text":"1. The restrained target is a servant in worship, so the conflict is obedience against obedience. Evidence: `ع ب د B003` worship/submissive obedience, `ع ب د B001` owned/slave status, `ص ل و B003` prescribed worship. Structural cue: 96:9's object is delayed until this short ayah, and the servant is indefinite `عبداً`, emphasizing role over personal identity. Mechanism: the transgressor forbids a servant precisely when the servant performs the embodied act of worship. Abductive move: the packet supplies servant/worship branches; I infer that the scene centers on competing claims over whom obedience belongs to. Reading change: the ayah is not just “a man praying”; it is an owned/devoted status under challenge. 2. `صلى` activates supplication/mercy and not only ritual form. Evidence: `ص ل و B002` supplication, praise, mercy; `ر ح م B001` mercy/tenderness; `ق ر ء B006` devout learned reader. Structural cue: the worship scene follows teaching and reading; the praying servant may be the embodied reader. Mechanism: recitation, teaching, and prayer form one devotional circuit of receiving and responding. Abductive move: the packet gives prayer as supplication/mercy and the opening mercy frame; I infer that the forbidden act is a channel of mercy, not merely posture. Reading change: obstructing prayer becomes obstruction of mercy-bearing response. 3. A darker branch turns prayer into an exposed snare-zone. Evidence: `ص ل و B004` set snare, activated by `ع ل ق B011` game caught in snare and 96:15 seizure by forelock. Structural cue: the servant is caught “when he prays,” at the vulnerable moment of obedience. Mechanism: the opponent treats devotional exposure as an opportunity to trap; the later divine seizure reverses the trap. Abductive move: the packet supplies snare branches but not an explicit trap; I infer this as a secondary mechanism from proximity to obstruction and later coercion. Reading change: the prayer scene also reads as a vulnerability that provokes attempted capture.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003","root_000973/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7ee46cb74bd0a765585a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:10:1\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kulun seçerek boyun eğmesi, engelleyen kişinin sonradan zorla boyun eğdirilmesinin karşıtıdır; aynı alçalma ekseninde irade tersine döner.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_98171a243d983e2596d7","text":"3. **Voluntary lowering opposed to coerced subduing.** `ع ب د:B003` assigns submissive obedience to worship, while `ع ب د:B005` supplies physical subduing and smoothing. The servant freely enters prayer; by 96:15 the restrainer faces coercive seizure by the forelock. The packet supplies the two bodily relations, and I infer the contrast in agency: one lowering is chosen devotion, the other imposed humiliation. The fixed `عبداً` consequently distinguishes willing service from the controller’s attempt to make another humanly subdued.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b1b62c444e4c799f92b0","text":"4. **The occasion treated as a snare, then capture reversed (exploratory).** `ص ل و:B004` supplies a set snare, a materially distinct branch from prescribed prayer. The restrainer acts specifically `إذا صلى`, as though prayer supplies the occasion on which to catch or block the servant; 96:15 then redirects capture to the restrainer’s forelock. I infer the snare relation from timing and reversal, not from an explicit trap noun. The dormant branch makes the scene read as a failed ambush whose logic returns upon its initiator.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bd1e585be9de2674df68","text":"1. **A servant located inside contested ownership.** `ع ب د:B001` supplies owned status, `ع ب د:B003` worshipful submission, and `ع ب د:B004` the act of reducing someone to slave-like subjection. The servant is the object of another human’s restraint (96:9), yet his act is prayer and return is to the Rabb (96:8). The three branches separate two jurisdictions: devotional belonging to the Rabb and attempted human control of that belonging. The packet gives the prohibition; I infer that it tries to redirect the servant’s obedience. `عبداً` therefore names not social insignificance but the disputed locus of authority.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d3d3999d6dac16065990","text":"96:19 changed this ayah by answering forbidden prayer with commanded prostration. The servant's prayer is not abandoned because it is dangerous; it is intensified into `واسجد واقترب`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001","root_000973/B003","root_000973/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_df9551d0f8a567b1ccf2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:10:1\",\"root_000973\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kul, Rabbine gönüllü bağlılık ile bir insanın onu kendine bağımlı kılma girişiminin çekiştiği otorite alanıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f156196e7e205a0b7eaf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:10:3\",\"root_000879\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Namaz anının özellikle seçilmesi, ibadetin kulu yakalamak için kurulmuş bir tuzak fırsatı sayıldığı, fakat yakalamanın sonunda engelleyene döndüğü ihtimalini açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B002","root_000879/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f9e94c964c3dd2fb3a4e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:10:3\",\"root_000879\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Namaz, belirlenmiş bedensel ibadet olmanın yanında baskı altında yöneltilen dua ve merhamet isteğidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
